{"session": "01a0f78b-98a3-7e41-9c67-c1340c68f96e", "timestamp": "2026-10-01T13:06:50.482Z", "call": {"type": "custom_tool_call", "id": "ctc_047b44164fa44362016abe5a4d250087d2b48d17aaf91a7def", "status": "completed", "call_id": "call_VJ8sgTA7WhZFKoLywXWSwADO", "name": "exec", "input": "text(await tools.apply_patch(\"*** Begin Patch\\n*** Add File: /private/var/folders/cs/vgp_x_353zd9frkjpysp7zdw0000gn/T/kntnt-native-session-r95dqkak/scratch/write-source-check-phwbhm4c/report-2.md\\n+# Source and quotation comparison\\n+\\n+Read `source.md` and the complete supplied `draft.md`, including headline, standfirst, byline and subheadings. Source language: Swedish. Target language: British English. This report concerns source support only. The byline identifies the source brief’s author, not the user.\\n+\\n+## Complete claim accounting\\n+\\n+| Draft passage | Source passage, in its original language | Comparison |\\n+| --- | --- | --- |\\n+| “Lervik should test before ending telephone bookings” | “Skriv en debattartikel för invånarna i fiktiva Lervik. Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” | Supported advocacy. The title retains testing before removal; the body supplies the permanence, duration, board and two-channel qualifications. |\\n+| “Booking totals in Lervik’s pilot report do not measure residents’ ability to book online.” | “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.” | Supported contextual conclusion about what the totals measure. No individual or population inability is inferred. The report is the named source. |\\n+| “The municipal executive board must weigh staff costs against the value of telephone access” | “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” | Supported as Sanna’s own advocacy in her article. “Must weigh” states her requested decision standard; it does not assert that a comparison has occurred. Telephone access identifies the retained channel whose user value is at issue in the brief. |\\n+| “yet the documents contain neither a time measurement nor a calculation of financial savings.” | “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” | Same document scope and absence of the specified measurements. The draft does not convert missing calculations into zero savings. |\\n+| “By Sanna Ek, spokesperson for Öppna beslut” | “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut.” | Name and role match. “Spokesperson” adds no natural-gender assertion. |\\n+| “The administrative report for Lervik’s municipal executive board meeting on 18 June proposes removing telephone booking from all seven venues for associations from September.” | “Tjänsteutlåtandet inför kommunstyrelsens möte den 18 juni föreslår att telefonbokning tas bort för alla sju lokaler från september.” Also: “ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler” | The same report is identified by its meeting and proposal. Date, proposal modality, seven-premises scope and September commencement match. “Administrative report” is a generic English description of the supplied document; the identifying details prevent substitution of a different report. “Venues for associations” identifies the supplied bookable association premises in this context, without asserting any additional event, size or ownership. |\\n+| “I do not oppose digital booking.” | “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning.” | Supported. Third-person reporting becomes the commissioned author’s first-person statement. No stronger endorsement is claimed. |\\n+| “But on behalf of Öppna beslut, I urge the board to postpone a permanent switch and test telephone and web booking together for six months.” | “Avsändare är Sanna Ek, talesperson för föreningen Öppna beslut. Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” | Author, representative capacity, requested board action, permanence, both channels and six-month duration are supported. A proposal remains a proposal. |\\n+| “Bookings tell us too little about users” | “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer. Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.” | Supported author evaluation of the evidence’s usefulness for this decision. It does not characterise 24 or 96 as a high or low count. |\\n+| “The municipality’s pilot report, dated 8 April 2026, records 96 web bookings and 24 telephone bookings during an eight-week trial at two venues.” | “Kommunens pilotrapport Bokning av föreningslokaler, 8 april 2026, gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler. Under försöket gjordes 96 bokningar via webb och 24 via telefon.” | Correct named attribution, date, duration, two-premises scope, route and counts. No people count, unique-user count, causal effect or extrapolation is added. |\\n+| “Those are bookings, not unique people.” | “Rapporten räknar bokningar, inte unika personer.” | Exact unit and exclusion preserved. |\\n+| “The report measures neither age nor functional ability nor familiarity with digital technology.” | “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” | Age and functional ability match. “Familiarity with digital technology” can cover knowledge without practical accustomedness, broadening the third measurement exclusion. Source-support finding F1 below. |\\n+| “So the telephone figure cannot tell us what proportion of Lervik’s residents cannot book online.” | “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana. Telefonbokningarna kan därför inte användas för att säga hur stor andel av invånarna som inte kan boka digitalt.” | The logical qualification “därför” and population scope are preserved. “Cannot” is warranted, rather than weakened to an unsupported possibility. |\\n+| “Nor does choosing the telephone prove an inability to use the web. It gives us a reason to ask why that route is still used.” | “Sanna Eks ståndpunkt: ”Att en bokning går via telefon är inte ett bevis för att någon inte kan använda webben. Det är ett skäl att ta reda på varför telefonen fortfarande används.” Detta får citeras eller refereras.” | Supported paraphrase of the author’s stated position. “Choosing” is used in a general evidential proposition; it does not describe a particular person’s choice or invent a reason for it. The claim that a telephone booking does not establish inability also supports the narrower proposition about a chosen telephone booking. Continued telephone use is in the source. |\\n+| “Staff time needs a fair comparison” | “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” | Supported normative heading. “Fair” describes the author’s desired comparison, not a measured result or a claim of dishonest conduct. |\\n+| “The administration’s objection to keeping both routes is the administrative work across two channels: staff enter information in two workflows.” | “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna. Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” | The objection and its explanation remain attributed to the administration in this sentence. The colon explains that stated objection; it is not a separately sourced observation of staff work. Both workflows and double administration are supplied. No quantified workload, saving, laziness or expense attributed to users is added. |\\n+| “That concern deserves to be taken seriously. But the documents provide no measurement of the time involved or calculation of financial savings.” | “Förvaltningens verkliga invändning mot att behålla båda kanalerna är dubbel administration, inte att telefonanvändare är lata eller dyra.” Also: “Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” | First sentence is the author’s evaluation of the supported objection. Second preserves the documentary limitation, without claiming there is no administrative cost or saving. |\\n+| “Removing a route before establishing its value to users and the work it requires leaves the board without the comparison it needs. Administrative simplicity is a reason to investigate the balance, not a substitute for doing so.” | “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also: “Motivet i utlåtandet är att personalen ska slippa föra in uppgifter i två flöden. Någon tidsmätning eller ekonomisk besparingsberäkning finns inte i handlingarna.” | Author’s argument about the requirements of a decision and an expressly conditional sequence. It does not claim a trial has proved a benefit, assign a fabricated motive, or assert a measured technical effect. The comparison and the stated administrative concern have source support. |\\n+| “A wider trial can inform the choice” | “Kommunens pilotrapport … gäller ett åtta veckor långt försök i två lokaler.” [The omitted words are the supplied report title and date, not a qualification.] “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” Also: “Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” | “Wider” has the explicit two-versus-seven scope comparison. “Can inform” expresses the supported proposal’s purpose and possibility; it guarantees no result. |\\n+| “Öppna beslut proposes keeping both booking routes for six months at all seven venues.” | “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” | Proposer, proposal status, both channels, duration and full seven-premises scope match. |\\n+| “During that trial, the administration should record the time spent on each route and ask users to say voluntarily why they choose telephone or web booking.” | “Under försöket ska förvaltningen registrera tidsåtgång per bokningsväg och be användarna frivilligt ange varför de väljer telefon eller webb.” | Proposed administration action, timing, per-route unit, users’ voluntary participation, and question about reasons all match. “Should” fits the proposal and does not imply an enacted instruction or future certainty. |\\n+| “We make no claim to have financed or costed that trial.” | “Föreningen gör inget anspråk på att ha finansierat eller kostnadsberäknat försöket.” | Exact epistemic limitation preserved. The draft does not claim that financing or costing has not happened. |\\n+| “The executive board must consider its cost. Asking for better evidence does not remove the need to decide what gathering it is worth.” | “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” | First sentence preserves the board’s responsibility and unresolved cost. Second is the author’s reflection on that requirement; it supplies no price, affordability finding, funding commitment or estimated saving. |\\n+| “Evidence should guide the final booking decision” | “Hon menar att kommunen först måste kunna väga arbetskostnaden mot värdet för användarna.” Also: “Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” | Supported author advocacy about the decision standard. No completed final decision is asserted. |\\n+| “Lervik’s municipal executive board should now postpone the permanent switch and agree a six-month trial of both routes, with its cost explicitly considered.” | “Tes: kommunstyrelsen bör skjuta upp ett permanent byte till enbart digital bokning av föreningslokaler och först pröva ett halvår med både telefon och webb.” Also: “Öppna beslut föreslår ett sex månader långt försök i alla sju lokaler, med båda bokningsvägarna kvar.” And: “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till.” | Supported present advocacy, naming the board’s possible decision and retaining cost uncertainty. “Now” is the article’s appeal at the proposed decision point, not a new dated event. |\\n+| “After that, it can decide whether to remove, change or retain a booking channel.” | “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till. Därefter kan den avgöra om en kanal ska tas bort, ändras eller behållas.” | Board referent, subsequent decision, possibility rather than promised outcome, singular channel and all three options are preserved. In context the source’s proposed sequence includes the trial and consideration of cost; the preceding draft sentence likewise preserves both. |\\n+\\n+## Pronouns, person, number and natural gender\\n+\\n+| Draft form | Account |\\n+| --- | --- |\\n+| “I” in the two first-person author statements | First person, singular, no natural gender. Referent is Sanna Ek under “Avsändare är Sanna Ek” and the byline. This is the commissioned author’s voice. Source “Hon motsätter sig inte digital bokning” explicitly attributes the position to her; the English first-person form does not reproduce or add a third-person gender assertion. |\\n+| “We” in “We make no claim” | First person, plural, no natural gender. Organisational voice on behalf of Öppna beslut, supported by the spokesperson brief and “Föreningen gör inget anspråk …”. It expresses the association’s position, not an invented assertion about the user or a counted membership. |\\n+| “us” in the heading and explanatory argument | First person, plural, no natural gender. Rhetorical inclusion of author and readers in evaluating evidence. No shared personal experience, actual knowledge, action or measured reader belief is claimed. The source’s intended readers are “invånarna i fiktiva Lervik”. |\\n+| “Those” | Plural demonstrative referring to the 96 and 24 bookings. Source explicitly counts “bokningar, inte unika personer”. It asserts no number of distinct humans. |\\n+| “they” in “why they choose” | Third person, plural, gender-neutral, referring to users asked during the proposed trial. Source has plural “användarna … varför de väljer telefon eller webb”. Both referent and grammatical number are supplied; no natural gender is inferred. |\\n+| “It” in “It gives us a reason” | Third person, singular, nonhuman antecedent: telephone booking/using that route. Matches “Det är ett skäl …”. No natural-gender assertion. |\\n+| “its” in “its value”, “it” in “the work it requires” | Third person, singular, nonhuman booking-route referent. Source discusses the retained telephone/web channels and weighing labour cost against user value. No human or gender assertion. |\\n+| “its cost” in the two trial-cost passages | Third person, singular, nonhuman trial referent. Source explicitly gives “försöket” and “Kostnaden behöver kommunstyrelsen ta ställning till”. No claim that the cost is known. |\\n+| “it” in “it can decide” | Third person, singular, nonhuman institutional referent: municipal executive board. Source “kommunstyrelsen … Därefter kan den avgöra …” supplies that referent and institutional singular. Swedish grammatical common gender in “den” does not become a human natural-gender claim. |\\n+\\n+Other demonstratives (“that route”, “That concern”, “that trial”, “After that”) refer respectively to telephone booking, the stated double-administration objection, the proposed six-month trial, and the preceding proposed sequence. Their referents are supplied; they carry no natural gender. “Both” and “each” refer to the two supplied channels, telephone and web. The draft introduces no he/she pronoun or other natural-gender assertion about any person. Sanna’s name alone has not been treated as gender evidence.\\n+\\n+## Findings\\n+\\n+### F1 — Source-support: measurement exclusion broadened across languages\\n+\\n+Draft: “The report measures neither age nor functional ability nor **familiarity with digital technology**.”\\n+\\n+Source: “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller **digital vana**.”\\n+\\n+The third term changes what is excluded from measurement. “Digital vana” concerns being accustomed to using digital technology. “Familiarity with digital technology” can also mean knowing about it without practical accustomedness. The source therefore does not establish every measurement absence the English sentence can assert.\\n+\\n+**Source-true/draft-false case:** the pilot report could include a test of users’ knowledge of digital tools learned from printed manuals, while measuring neither their practical accustomedness to using those tools, age, nor functional ability. It could still count bookings rather than unique people. Such a report would satisfy the supplied exclusions but would measure a form of familiarity. No supplied statement excludes that case; the source does not describe every other possible measure in the report.\\n+\\n+**Reverse-direction test:** a report measuring users’ habitual digital use while omitting a separate knowledge assessment could distinguish practical accustomedness from knowledge-based familiarity. As a case for this actual report, however, it is excluded by the explicit statement “Den mäter inte ålder, funktionsförmåga eller digital vana.” The surviving first-direction case is sufficient to establish the draft’s broader absence claim.\\n+\\n+**Smallest supported repair:** “The report measures neither age nor functional ability nor **how accustomed users are to using digital technology**.” This retains the measured attributes, certainty and report attribution while narrowing the third term to the supplied one.\\n+\\n+No other source-support defect was found. The administration’s explanation remains attributed in its complete sentence; the report’s counts and exclusions remain qualified; the organisation’s absence of a claim is not turned into knowledge of non-occurrence; and the author’s advocacy is not treated as an empirical finding.\\n+\\n+## Quotation and translation findings\\n+\\n+There is no direct speech quotation in the draft. The supplied quotation is paraphrased with its evidential point and author attribution preserved. There is no translated-quotation finding. F1 concerns the source support of non-quoted prose, rather than quotation voice or idiom. General editorial and mechanical review were not undertaken.\\n+\\n+Completion status: complete. Unresolved findings: F1 remains in the supplied draft; the supported repair above has not been applied because this task authorises only the report.\\n*** End Patch\"));\n", "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {"turn_id": "01a0f78b-98d1-7d21-8799-e3174dcc301b", "create_time": 1790859774.530852}}}
